# Snake-pit DNS Sinkhole blocklist
# Syncs to SD at /andraste/snake-pit/blocklist.txt (via SD Setup / sdmanifest.json).
# One domain per line. Lines starting with # are comments.
# Blocking a domain also covers all subdomains (ads.youtube.com is covered by youtube.com).
# Subdomains listed individually here are for ad/tracker CDNs that differ from the parent brand.
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------
